<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Rhododendron A–Z (Accepted + Synonyms)</title>
  <style>
    body { font-family: system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .auth { color: #555; }
    .pub { color: #666; font-size: 0.85em; }
    .nom { color: #444; font-size: 0.85em; }
    .syn { color: #555; margin-left: 3em; }
    .infra { margin-left: 1.5em; }
    .status { color: #b00; font-size: 0.85em; }
    ul { list-style-type: none; padding-left: 0; }
    li { margin: 0.25em 0; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
<h1>Rhododendron — A–Z of Accepted Names with Synonyms</h1>
<ul>
  <li><strong>Rhododendron aberconwayi</strong> <span class="auth">Cowan</span> — <span class="pub">Rhododendron Year Book 1948: 42 (1948)</span>; <span class="nom">Valid</span>
    <ul>
      <li class="syn">Rhododendron maxiongense <span class="auth">C.Q.Zhang &amp; D.Paterson</span> — <span class="pub">Novon 13: 156 (2003)</span>; <span class="nom">Valid</span> <span class="status">[synonym]</span></li>
    </ul>
  </li>
  <li><strong>Rhododendron abietifolium</strong> <span class="auth">Sleumer</span> — <span class="pub">Blumea 11: 122 (1961)</span>; <span class="nom">Valid</span>
  </li>
  <li><strong>Rhododendron acrophilum</strong> <span class="auth">Merr. &amp; Quisumb.</span> — <span class="pub">Philipp. J. Sci. 82: 333 (1954)</span>; <span class="nom">Valid</span>
  </li>
  <li><strong>Rhododendron acuminatum</strong> <span class="auth">Hook.f.</span> — <span class="pub">Hooker's Icon. Pl. 9: t. 886 (1851)</span>; <span class="nom">Valid</span>
  </li>
  <li><strong>Rhododendron adamsii</strong> <span class="auth">Rehder</span> — <span class="pub">Monogr. Azaleas [E.H. Wilson &amp; A. Rehder] 190 (1921)</span>; <span class="nom">Valid</span>
    <ul>
      <li class="syn">Azalea fragrans <span class="auth">Adams</span> — <span class="pub">Mém. Acad. Imp. Sci. St. Pétersbourg Hist. Acad. 2: 332 (1808)</span>; <span class="nom">Valid</span> <span class="status">[synonym]</span></li>
      <li class="syn">Azalea multiflora <span class="auth">Turcz. ex DC.</span> — <span class="pub">Prodr. 7: 715 (1839)</span>; <span class="nom">Valid</span> <span class="status">[synonym]</span></li>
      <li class="syn">Osmothamnus fragrans <span class="auth">(Adams) DC.</span> — <span class="pub">Prodr. 7: 715 (1839)</span>; <span class="nom">Valid</span> <span class="status">[synonym]</span></li>
      <li class="syn">Rhododendron fragrans <span class="auth">(Adams) Maxim.</span> — <span class="pub">Mém. Acad. Imp. Sci. Saint Pétersbourg, Sér. 7 16(9): 16 (1870)</span>; <span class="nom">Illegitimate</span> <span class="status">[synonym]</span></li>
